scripts/autoheal/autoheal_rules.jsonl:2:{"id":"AH-2026-03-27-1654-PHASE1_PROVIDER_ADAPTER_COMPAT-001","date":"2026-03-27","scope":"src/services/ai/types.ts,src/services/ai/providerFabric.ts,src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerFabricAdapter.test.ts","symptom":"Phase 1 canonical provider ids and adapter surface existed in docs/types but no bounded compatibility registry or conformance proof connected promoted providers to the adapter contract","root_cause":"1) AIProviderAdapter depended on AIProviderId values that still used legacy ids ('anthropic','local') instead of the frozen canonical ids ('claude','titane-local') 2) promoted providers remained on legacy AIProvider without a shared adapter shim 3) no focused conformance test covered the promoted adapter registry","fix":"Aligned AIProviderId with canonical promoted ids, added an additive provider-fabric compatibility adapter registry for the promoted set, and added targeted conformance tests without mutating existing provider runtime behavior","prevention_test":"Run provider fabric vitest slice, verify_instructions.sh, and autoheal/detect_recurrence.sh","commands":["pnpm exec vitest run src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerFabricAdapter.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/openai.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/claude.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerMemoryReuse.test.ts","bash scripts/verify_instructions.sh","bash scripts/autoheal/detect_recurrence.sh"],"files_changed":["src/services/ai/types.ts","src/services/ai/providerFabric.ts","src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerFabricAdapter.test.ts"],"rollback":"git restore -- src/services/ai/types.ts src/services/ai/providerFabric.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerFabricAdapter.test.ts scripts/autoheal/autoheal_rules.jsonl"}
scripts/autoheal/autoheal_rules.jsonl:3:{"id":"AH-2026-03-27-1713-PHASE1_PROVIDER_FABRIC_STATUS-001","date":"2026-03-27","scope":"src/services/ai/providerFabricCatalog.ts,src/services/ai/providerFabric.ts,src/services/ai/orchestrator.ts,src/services/ai/__tests__/providerFabricStatus.test.ts","symptom":"Phase 1 provider fabric had a bounded adapter registry but orchestrator status surfaces did not expose canonical promoted-provider metadata or truthful lazy-load visibility","root_cause":"1) provider metadata lived only inside the adapter shim and could not be consumed without importing live providers 2) orchestrator status surfaces only reported legacy provider stats 3) no focused proof covered truthful status for unloaded lazy providers","fix":"Added a metadata-only promoted provider catalog, reused it from the adapter layer, and wired orchestrator status snapshots to expose canonical promoted-provider fabric truth without forcing lazy cloud provider loads","prevention_test":"Run provider fabric status/orchestrator vitest slice, verify_instructions.sh, and autoheal/detect_recurrence.sh","commands":["pnpm exec vitest run src/services/ai/__tests__/providerFabricStatus.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerFabricAdapter.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/openai.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/claude.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerMemoryReuse.test.ts src/services/ai/__tests__/ollamaAbortFallback.test.ts","bash scripts/verify_instructions.sh","bash scripts/autoheal/detect_recurrence.sh"],"files_changed":["src/services/ai/providerFabricCatalog.ts","src/services/ai/providerFabric.ts","src/services/ai/orchestrator.ts","src/services/ai/__tests__/providerFabricStatus.test.ts"],"rollback":"git restore -- src/services/ai/providerFabricCatalog.ts src/services/ai/providerFabric.ts src/services/ai/orchestrator.ts src/services/ai/__tests__/providerFabricStatus.test.ts scripts/autoheal/autoheal_rules.jsonl"}
scripts/autoheal/autoheal_rules.jsonl:4:{"id":"AH-2026-03-27-1736-LOCK1_PROVIDER_FABRIC_STATUS_FIX-001","date":"2026-03-27","scope":"src/services/ai/orchestrator.ts,src/services/ai/types.ts","symptom":"Provider fabric status test failed because orchestrator snapshots lacked the promoted provider fabric array and AIProviderId still used legacy ids inconsistent with the Phase 1 canon","root_cause":"1) getProvidersStatus() never included the promoted provider fabric entries, leaving snapshot.fabric undefined 2) AIProviderId still listed legacy ids, drifting from the canonical promoted provider set","fix":"Added promoted provider fabric status to orchestrator snapshots without forcing lazy loads, and aligned AIProviderId to the canonical promoted ids","prevention_test":"Run provider fabric status/adapters vitest slice, verify_instructions.sh, and autoheal/detect_recurrence.sh","commands":["pnpm exec vitest run src/services/ai/__tests__/providerFabricStatus.test.ts src/services/ai/providers/__tests__/providerFabricAdapter.test.ts","bash scripts/verify_instructions.sh","bash scripts/autoheal/detect_recurrence.sh"],"files_changed":["src/services/ai/orchestrator.ts","src/services/ai/types.ts"],"rollback":"git restore -- src/services/ai/orchestrator.ts src/services/ai/types.ts scripts/autoheal/autoheal_rules.jsonl"}
